# Third-party notices

This file records the third-party components used by MeowChan's desktop application,
their licensing sources, and the full license/notice texts provided by upstream.
The inventory was checked on **2026-10-10** against the resolved Release package
metadata and a self-contained `linux-x64` publish using SDK `10.0.111`.
The SQLite entries were added afterward from the current Release lockfile and
NuGet archives (`Microsoft.Data.Sqlite` 10.0.12 / `SQLitePCLRaw` 2.1.12). Their
native assets were verified in locked publish restores for all five target RIDs
and in a new self-contained `linux-x64` publish. This is package evidence, not
native runtime acceptance on Windows or macOS.
The application itself is covered by the separate `LICENSE` file (Artistic
License 2.0); that license does not replace the dependency licenses below.

The inventory includes the native dependencies for the configured Windows,
Linux and macOS targets. A particular archive contains only its selected native
assets. Build-only packages, test packages and WebAssembly-only native assets are
not part of the desktop distribution. Upstream aggregate notice files are
preserved in full and can describe components beyond the subset used here.

## Desktop themes and controls (2026-10-22)

The 1.0 desktop uses Semi.Avalonia and Semi.Avalonia.DataGrid 12.1.0.1,
Irihi.Ursa and Irihi.Ursa.Themes.Semi 2.2.0, and their Irihi.Avalonia.Shared /
Irihi.Avalonia.Shared.Contracts 0.5.0 dependencies. The six resolved NuGet
archives each declare MIT and contain a full LICENSE file. Those texts and
original hashes appear below. Package author metadata is IRIHI Technology Co.,
Ltd.; copyright attribution is retained exactly as supplied in each LICENSE,
including the .NET Foundation attribution in the Ursa packages. The previous
proprietary theme assemblies are no longer dependencies or distributed assets.

## Bundled visual assets

- `Assets/icons/*.png`: Feather Icons, attributed by the application's existing
  About window. The original export revision is unknown. The verified upstream
  MIT text and Cole Bemis attribution are included below.
- `Avalonia.Fonts.Inter`: Inter font version `3.019;git-0a5106e0b`, verified from
  the font metadata at the package source commit. Font files are covered by SIL
  Open Font License 1.1; the Avalonia wrapper's MIT declaration does not replace
  that font license.
- `Assets/meowchan.png`, `Assets/meowchan.ico` and high-resolution source
  `Assets/branding/meowchan-source.png`: cat-head branding selected by the
  project maintainer on 2026-10-10, derived with OpenAI ImageGen from a cat
  photograph supplied by the maintainer. The PNG, Windows ICO and packaged
  macOS ICNS use the same selected photographic cutout.

## Gallery video dependencies (2026-10-20)

The desktop application additionally uses the following VideoLAN packages. All
four NuSpec files declare `LGPL-2.1-or-later`. The package archives contain no
standalone LICENSE, COPYING or aggregate third-party notice file. The complete
upstream LGPL 2.1 text is reproduced below. Linux uses the distribution's system
LibVLC and does not redistribute a native VLC tree in the application package.

| Package | Version | NuGet archive SHA-256 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| LibVLCSharp | 3.10.2 | `a7473e6d7c3595c74d3fb42bb0791053224d07b797adcee8c16f00e6f99bb8f9` |
| LibVLCSharp.Avalonia | 3.10.2 | `ece52a2fb9cfbb944639e189cf81cd54f50a7f1ebed04d685b20ab088027d533` |
| VideoLAN.LibVLC.Windows | 3.0.24 | `3da3f37ee2b2b10e5c788d830a3b513658c36521ea1b0f966ed86eb69f41ef3c` |
| VideoLAN.LibVLC.Mac | 3.1.3.2 | `b83faf0e4866a5d8c53e998aec4caafc38d0e8aff9390a2a02d49af4d9757715` |

Copyright and project attribution: VideoLAN and LibVLCSharp contributors.
Source projects: [LibVLCSharp](https://code.videolan.org/videolan/LibVLCSharp),
[LibVLC native packaging](https://code.videolan.org/videolan/libvlc-nuget),
[VLC](https://code.videolan.org/videolan/vlc), and
[VLC source downloads](https://download.videolan.org/pub/videolan/vlc/).
The Windows exact-source mapping, collected notices and companion-source asset
are documented below; the generic upstream links alone are not that mechanism.

Both selected native core binaries report VLC `3.0.24-rc1-0-g6de05adcba`;
the macOS NuGet version `3.1.3.2` is a package version, not VLC's core version.
The [exact VLC source revision](https://code.videolan.org/videolan/vlc/-/tree/6de05adcba)
and its [COPYING.LIB](https://code.videolan.org/videolan/vlc/-/blob/6de05adcba/COPYING.LIB)
identify the native core source/license. The official
[VLC 3.0.24 source archive](https://download.videolan.org/pub/videolan/vlc/3.0.24/vlc-3.0.24.tar.xz)
has SHA-256 `e7cab503d1d7d5849b89d2cf0e1ee60d0ef6d012407791b644b9cfc0cc225fdf`,
checked against the [published checksum](https://download.videolan.org/pub/videolan/vlc/3.0.24/vlc-3.0.24.tar.xz.sha256).
Its `contrib/src` directory contains component download versions, SHA-512
checksums, build recipes and patches; upstream mirrors component source archives
at [VideoLAN contrib downloads](https://download.videolan.org/pub/contrib/).
The native NuGet upstream [Windows packaging script](https://code.videolan.org/videolan/libvlc-nuget/-/blob/master/package-nuget-win.sh)
and [Mac packaging script](https://code.videolan.org/videolan/libvlc-nuget/-/blob/master/package-nuget-mac.sh)
repackage official VLC distributions and remove enumerated modules. The Mac
package retains four modules whose exact source headers declare GPL-2.0-or-later:
`libdolby_surround_decoder_plugin.dylib`, `libheadphone_channel_mixer_plugin.dylib`,
`libsyslog_plugin.dylib` and `liblua_plugin.dylib`, in both architectures. The
NuSpec aggregate LGPL expression does not override those individual licenses.
Their source locations, hashes, full GPL v2 text and the verified 800-file
NuGet-to-official-DMG mapping are recorded in the [Mac source notices and
manifest](docs/native-video-macos-sources.md). The complete source base includes
these modules. No plugin removal or change to MeowChan's Artistic 2.0 license is
implied. The pinned upstream packaging helper accompanies application source.

To use a compatible rebuilt native library, quit MeowChan, back up the complete
`libvlc/<RID>` directory, replace that tree while preserving its plugin hierarchy
and CPU architecture, then restart. On macOS the directory is inside
`MeowChan.app/Contents/Resources/application/libvlc/<RID>` for signed packages,
exposed through a relative `Contents/MacOS/libvlc` link (legacy unsigned packages
keep it directly in `Contents/MacOS`). Modifying a bundle requires re-signing the
modified bundle for local use; see the [Mac build/replacement instructions](docs/native-video-macos-sources.md).
To rebuild the managed binding, use the LibVLCSharp 3.10.2 source
and replace its package reference with the rebuilt compatible package before
building MeowChan. The desktop project and Release locks record all binding inputs.

The native libraries and plugin trees remain separate, replaceable files beside
the application, including in single-file .NET publishes. Users may replace these
LGPL components with compatible modified versions. The application license does
not restrict reverse engineering for debugging modifications to these components.
The binding source is available upstream and the application source includes the
integration and pinned package references.

For the Windows prerelease, the concrete source/notice mechanism is recorded in
[Windows video source availability](docs/native-video-sources.md). Its
[component license texts](docs/native-video-licenses.txt) and
[source-input manifest](docs/native-video-source-manifest.json) accompany the
application. Upload `MeowChan-0.9.0-video-sources.tar.gz` to the **same GitHub
prerelease as the Windows binary ZIP**, and include its hash in the release
checksums. That companion supplies the verified official VLC source, the pinned
contrib source inputs/patches and LibVLCSharp sources; merely publishing this
paragraph without the companion asset does not complete the source mechanism.
For a Mac release, offer that same immutable source base together with the
release-specific overlay described in [Mac source availability](docs/native-video-macos-sources.md),
bound to the final per-RID ZIP hashes. The source inventory is separate from
exact upstream binary rebuild provenance and [native signing/runtime acceptance](docs/macos-signing.md).

## Maintaining this inventory

When a package or the SDK-selected self-contained runtime changes, compare the
new package metadata and bundled licenses with this inventory and refresh the
corresponding text. The final artifact check must confirm that `LICENSE` and
`THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md` accompany every distributed desktop archive and that the
notice set matches that archive's runtime and native packages. This document is
licensing evidence and attribution; it does not assert that release acceptance
or platform validation has been completed.

The sections below reproduce upstream text with UTF-8 BOMs removed, line endings
normalized, and trailing line whitespace removed. Each SHA-256 identifies the
original downloaded/package bytes before that formatting normalization.

## Feather Icons — MIT

Source: [LICENSE](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/feathericons/feather/1b002399e8758fb2bccea4d07312dc9e0f43dcb8/LICENSE).

Original SHA-256: `308028e93fcf84972523cdf6e616f73168546b4953895f516d01287f16fe7bee`.

Applies to `Assets/icons/*.png`, `Assets/icons/feather/*.svg`, and the derived
`Assets/icons/feather/masks/*.png`. The new `feather/` SVGs are unchanged originals
from Feather v4.29.2, commit `1b002399e8758fb2bccea4d07312dc9e0f43dcb8`; their
48 × 48 PNG alpha masks are local raster derivatives. The original MIT license
also accompanies those assets as `Assets/icons/feather/LICENSE`.

The existing About window identifies Feather as the source of the older
`Assets/icons/*.png`. Their original PNG export revision is not recorded;
v4.29.2 is the verified upstream license source, not a claim about their exporter
version. The pinned source of the new `feather/` assets does not change that
historical limitation.

--- LICENSE.TXT ---

The  FreeType 2  font  engine is  copyrighted  work and  cannot be  used
legally  without a  software license.   In  order to  make this  project
usable  to a vast  majority of  developers, we  distribute it  under two
mutually exclusive open-source licenses.

This means  that *you* must choose  *one* of the  two licenses described
below, then obey  all its terms and conditions when  using FreeType 2 in
any of your projects or products.

  - The FreeType License, found in  the file `FTL.TXT', which is similar
    to the original BSD license *with* an advertising clause that forces
    you  to  explicitly cite  the  FreeType  project  in your  product's
    documentation.  All  details are in the license  file.  This license
    is  suited  to products  which  don't  use  the GNU  General  Public
    License.

    Note that  this license  is  compatible  to the  GNU General  Public
    License version 3, but not version 2.

  - The GNU General Public License version 2, found in  `GPLv2.TXT' (any
    later version can be used  also), for programs which already use the
    GPL.  Note  that the  FTL is  incompatible  with  GPLv2 due  to  its
    advertisement clause.

The contributed BDF and PCF drivers  come with a license similar to that
of the X Window System.  It is compatible to the above two licenses (see
file src/bdf/README and  src/pcf/README).  The same holds  for the files
`fthash.c' and  `fthash.h'; their  code was  part of  the BDF  driver in
earlier FreeType versions.

The gzip module uses the zlib license (see src/gzip/zlib.h) which too is
compatible to the above two licenses.

The MD5 checksum support (only used for debugging in development builds)
is in the public domain.


--- end of LICENSE.TXT ---

  5. Time Zone Database

   ICU uses the public domain data and code derived from Time Zone Database
   for its time zone support. The ownership of the TZ database is explained
   in BCP 175: Procedure for Maintaining the Time Zone Database section 7.

7.  Database Ownership

   The TZ database itself is not an IETF Contribution or an IETF
   document.  Rather it is a pre-existing and regularly updated work
   that is in the public domain, and is intended to remain in the public
   domain.  Therefore, BCPs 78 [RFC5378] and 79 [RFC3979] do not apply
   to the TZ Database or contributions that individuals make to it.
   Should any claims be made and substantiated against the TZ Database,
   the organization that is providing the IANA Considerations defined in
   this RFC, under the memorandum of understanding with the IETF,
   currently ICANN, may act in accordance with all competent court
   orders.  No ownership claims will be made by ICANN or the IETF Trust
   on the database or the code.  Any person making a contribution to the
   database or code waives all rights to future claims in that
   contribution or in the TZ Database.

################################################################################
# libjpeg-turbo
################################################################################

libjpeg-turbo Licenses
======================

libjpeg-turbo is covered by three compatible BSD-style open source licenses:

- The IJG (Independent JPEG Group) License, which is listed in
  [README.ijg](README.ijg)

  This license applies to the libjpeg API library and associated programs
  (any code inherited from libjpeg, and any modifications to that code.)

- The Modified (3-clause) BSD License, which is listed in
  [turbojpeg.c](turbojpeg.c)

  This license covers the TurboJPEG API library and associated programs.

- The zlib License, which is listed in [simd/jsimdext.inc](simd/jsimdext.inc)

  This license is a subset of the other two, and it covers the libjpeg-turbo
  SIMD extensions.


Complying with the libjpeg-turbo Licenses
=========================================

This section provides a roll-up of the libjpeg-turbo licensing terms, to the
best of our understanding.

1.  If you are distributing a modified version of the libjpeg-turbo source,
    then:

    1.  You cannot alter or remove any existing copyright or license notices
        from the source.

        **Origin**
        - Clause 1 of the IJG License
        - Clause 1 of the Modified BSD License
        - Clauses 1 and 3 of the zlib License

    2.  You must add your own copyright notice to the header of each source
        file you modified, so others can tell that you modified that file (if
        there is not an existing copyright header in that file, then you can
        simply add a notice stating that you modified the file.)

        **Origin**
        - Clause 1 of the IJG License
        - Clause 2 of the zlib License

    3.  You must include the IJG README file, and you must not alter any of the
        copyright or license text in that file.

        **Origin**
        - Clause 1 of the IJG License

2.  If you are distributing only libjpeg-turbo binaries without the source, or
    if you are distributing an application that statically links with
    libjpeg-turbo, then:

    1.  Your product documentation must include a message stating:

        This software is based in part on the work of the Independent JPEG
        Group.

        **Origin**
        - Clause 2 of the IJG license

    2.  If your binary distribution includes or uses the TurboJPEG API, then
        your product documentation must include the text of the Modified BSD
        License.

        **Origin**
        - Clause 2 of the Modified BSD License

3.  You cannot use the name of the IJG or The libjpeg-turbo Project or the
    contributors thereof in advertising, publicity, etc.

    **Origin**
    - IJG License
    - Clause 3 of the Modified BSD License

4.  The IJG and The libjpeg-turbo Project do not warrant libjpeg-turbo to be
    free of defects, nor do we accept any liability for undesirable
    consequences resulting from your use of the software.

    **Origin**
    - IJG License
    - Modified BSD License
    - zlib License

## SQLitePCLRaw — Apache License 2.0

The `bundle_e_sqlite3`, `core`, `lib.e_sqlite3` and `provider.e_sqlite3`
2.1.12 NuSpec files declare Apache-2.0, with package attribution
`Copyright 2014-2024 SourceGear, LLC`. None of these NuGet archives contains a
separate license/notice text. The following full text comes from the matching
upstream `v2.1.12` tag:
https://github.com/ericsink/SQLitePCL.raw/blob/v2.1.12/LICENSE.TXT

`lib.e_sqlite3` provides native SQLite binaries, including the configured
Windows x64, Linux x64/Arm64 and macOS x64/Arm64 assets. SQLite itself is in the
public domain according to its upstream copyright statement:
https://www.sqlite.org/copyright.html
This is distinct from the Apache-2.0 declaration on the SQLitePCLRaw package.
